## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:257 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L257) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: catalog
TL;DR: the cap warning counts as missing up to four seed realms the preview actually rendered
Check: BuildPlan with gnoweb changed, maxRealms below the affected count and gno.land/r/gnoland/home among the truncated affected realms: expect Dropped to exclude it, observe it counted while present in plan.Realms
Details: plan.go:256-258 sets plan.Dropped = len(realms) - maxRealms and truncates, then plan.go:263-269 appends any gnowebSeedRealms absent from the truncated slice back into plan.Realms without touching Dropped. comment.go:85-86 prints that stale count as N more affected realm(s) were not rendered (cap reached), so a realm sitting in the preview is reported as lost, by up to len(gnowebSeedRealms). It needs plan.Gnoweb set, more affected realms than maxRealms, and a seed in the truncated tail — reachable since the seeds sort under gno.land/r/docs/ and gno.land/r/gnoland/, after the gno.land/r/demo/ block. Fix: compute Dropped after the seed append, from the realms actually absent from plan.Realms.
Evidence: read plan.go:256-270: `plan.Dropped = len(realms) - maxRealms` and `realms = realms[:maxRealms]` precede `if plan.Gnoweb { for _, r := range gnowebSeedRealms { if pkgs[r] != nil && !contains(plan.Realms, r) { plan.Realms = append(plan.Realms, r) } } }`; no assignment to plan.Dropped exists after line 257, and comment.go:86 is its only reader.

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:264 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L264) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: catalog + claims + reach
TL;DR: a gnoweb PR can hand gnodev four realms more than the cap the flag names
Check: build a fake repo whose 25 changed realms sort before gno.land/r/docs/security_patterns, set Gnoweb, and assert len(plan.Realms) <= maxRealms | add pkg("examples/gno.land/r/gnoland/home", "gno.land/r/gnoland/home", ...) to fakeRepo and re-run go test ./misc/gnopreview -run TestBuildPlan: the "gnoweb alone" case must then assert the seed in wantRealms, and a maxRealms=1 gnoweb case must show Realms exceeding the cap | go test -run TestSeedRealmAffectedIsHiddenFromComment -v ./misc/gnopreview with the artifact copied in: a plan whose Realms are [gno.land/r/gnoland/home gno.land/r/x/other], both affected by the same changed package, and a Comment() that names only other. Cross-checked on the real tree with `gnopreview plan -root . -changed` on ownable.gno with and without gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/app.go.
Details: BuildPlan truncates realms to maxRealms and records Dropped at plan.go:256-259, then appends every gnowebSeedRealms entry not already kept at plan.go:263-270, with no second bound. The triggering input is concrete: a PR touching gno.land/pkg/gnoweb plus a package imported by more than maxRealms realms, none of them the four seeds — the cap keeps 25, then all four seeds append, giving len(plan.Realms) = 29. The finder's real-tree run did not exhibit it because it changed every gnomod.toml, which makes the seeds changed realms and so already kept. Fix: append the seeds before the truncation, or re-truncate after appending.
Evidence: plan.go:256-259 `if maxRealms > 0 && len(realms) > maxRealms { plan.Dropped = len(realms) - maxRealms; realms = realms[:maxRealms] }`; plan.go:263-268 `if plan.Gnoweb { for _, r := range gnowebSeedRealms { if pkgs[r] != nil && !contains(plan.Realms, r) { plan.Realms = append(plan.Realms, r) } } }`.

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:266 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L266) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: claims + lines
TL;DR: -max-realms is exceeded by up to four realms on any PR that touches gnoweb, and Dropped says nothing
Check: go test -run TestClaimSeedRealmsBypassCap in misc/gnopreview with a fake repo that contains the four gnowebSeedRealms: BuildPlan(root, ["gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/app.go", "examples/gno.land/p/x/base/v0/lib.gno"], 1) must return 1 realm; observe 5 | go test ./... -run Cap -v: BuildPlan with a gnoweb change, one changed realm and maxRealms=1 returns len(Realms)=5
Details: plan.go:256-258 applies the cap, and plan.go:263-268 then appends each of the four gnowebSeedRealms not already present, with no cap check, whenever plan.Gnoweb is set. Any diff under gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/, gno.land/cmd/gnoweb/ or contribs/gnodev/ sets that flag (plan.go:28-32, 212-216), so -max-realms=1 renders up to five realms and reports Dropped=0. The knob is what bounds gnodev boot and crawl time in the preview job, and the comment carries no sign of the overrun. Fix: append the seeds before the truncation, or re-apply the cap after appending them.
Evidence: read plan.go:256-270 — the append loop has no length guard and runs after `realms = realms[:maxRealms]`; the finder's run over a fake repo holding the four seeds gives `max-realms=1 rendered=5 dropped=0 realms=[gno.land/r/docs/security_patterns gno.land/r/gnoland/blog gno.land/r/gnoland/boards2/v0 gno.land/r/gnoland/home gno.land/r/x/leaf]`.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-lines-changed-realms-dropped-by-cap.go

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:153 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L153) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: reach
TL;DR: only the listener is closed, so the snapshot server's connections are left to the process exit
Check: read shots.go:153-163: srv is an *http.Server, the returned io.Closer is ln, and nothing calls srv.Close or srv.Shutdown
Details: serve at shots.go:153-163 builds the *http.Server at 158 and returns ln, the net.Listener, as its io.Closer; the server value escapes nowhere else in the package. Screenshot at shots.go:127 and ScreenshotPairs at shots.go:61 defer that Closer, so http.Server.Close — the call that closes tracked connections — never runs, and Chrome's keep-alive connection to the snapshot server plus the handler goroutine live until the process exits. The //nolint comment at shots.go:162 speaks to Serve's return value and says nothing about the server's connections. Fix: return an io.Closer whose Close calls srv.Close(), which closes the listener as its side effect.
Evidence: read shots.go:153-163 and grep for srv.Close/srv.Shutdown across misc/gnopreview: the only Close calls are `defer srv.Close()` at shots.go:61 and shots.go:127, where the identifier srv is bound to serve()'s first return value, the listener.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:163 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L163) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: catalog
TL;DR: serve hands back the listener, so http.Server.Close never runs and the Serve error is dropped
Check: read shots.go:153-163 against shots.go:61 and shots.go:127: the value named srv at the call sites is the listener, and no reference to the *http.Server survives serve().
Details: shots.go:158 builds srv := &http.Server{...}, shots.go:162 runs go srv.Serve(ln) with the error discarded, and shots.go:163 returns ln as the io.Closer, so no reference to the *http.Server survives serve(). The deferred closes at shots.go:61 and shots.go:127 therefore close the listener and never call http.Server.Close, which is what drains tracked connections. A Serve that fails after a successful bind is silent, and every chromeShot then reports Chrome's own connection error instead of the real cause. The process exits moments later, so nothing outlives it. Fix: return a Closer whose Close calls srv.Close(), and log the Serve error when it is not http.ErrServerClosed.
Evidence: read shots.go:153-163: `srv := &http.Server{Handler: http.FileServer(http.Dir(dir)), ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second}`, `go srv.Serve(ln) //nolint:errcheck`, `return ln, "http://" + ln.Addr().String(), nil`; callers at shots.go:61 and shots.go:127 are `defer srv.Close()` on that returned io.Closer, and grep for srv.Close/srv.Shutdown in the package finds no other site.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:195 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L195) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: reach
TL;DR: a -chrome path that does not resolve is ignored and the shots are taken with some other browser, silently
Check: read shots.go:195-213: the explicit candidate is one entry of the same slice as the fallbacks, with no error path when it fails both LookPath and Stat
Details: shots.go:196-200 puts the explicit flag and $CHROME into the same candidate slice as google-chrome, google-chrome-stable, chromium, chromium-browser and the macOS bundle, and the loop at shots.go:201-211 continues past any candidate failing both LookPath and Stat. A typo, or a path absent from the runner image, therefore falls through to whatever browser is installed, with nothing on stderr saying the pinned one was not used. The flag exists to pin a browser, so a version-specific rendering difference between the before and after columns becomes invisible. Fix: when explicit is non-empty and resolves to neither a PATH entry nor a file, return an error naming the path instead of falling through.
Evidence: read shots.go:195-213: `candidates := []string{explicit, os.Getenv("CHROME")}` then `candidates = append(candidates, "google-chrome", ...)`; the loop body is `if c == "" { continue }`, LookPath, Stat, and the function ends `return ""` — there is no branch distinguishing the explicit entry from the fallbacks.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:208 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L208) · Nit
State: CONFIRMED, band: Nit, angle: catalog + refactor
TL;DR: the os.Stat fallback hands back a browser path that cannot be executed, so a clean skip becomes one exec failure per page
Check: read shots.go:195-212: LookPath is the executability test and the Stat fallback has no IsExecutable check; confirm with touch /tmp/x && gnopreview render -chrome /tmp/x. | go doc os/exec.LookPath for the slash rule, then in a scratch dir: touch chromium && chmod -x chromium, and call findChrome("") with CHROME unset from the package's working directory; observe a non-empty return whose exec fails
Details: exec.LookPath resolves a slash-carrying path directly whenever it is runnable, so the -chrome flag, $CHROME and the macOS bundle path all reach the caller through the LookPath branch at shots.go:205. The os.Stat branch at :208 therefore fires only when the path exists, is not a directory, and LookPath refused it — no exec permission. findChrome returns it, bin != "", and every chromeShot then dies with permission denied, printing '  ! screenshot <url>: ...' once per page instead of the single '  ! no Chrome/Chromium found' line at :53 and :119. A bare name is worse: os.Stat resolves "chromium" against the working directory while exec.Command re-resolves a separator-free name on PATH, so the value returned cannot be run at all, even when the cwd file is executable. What the user loses is the diagnosis, not the preview — screenshots are best-effort and both routes end with no images. Fix: drop the os.Stat branch, or gate it on the executable bit and on the candidate carrying a path separator.
Evidence: Wrote judge-75-findchrome-stat-fallback_test.go into misc/gnopreview in a scratch worktree at ecf7af0f2 and ran go test -run TestJudge75 -v. All three pass at head. TestJudge75_LookPathCoversExecutableSlashedPath: a 0755 file named 'Google Chrome' comes back from exec.LookPath unchanged, so Stat is never consulted for a runnable candidate. TestJudge75_StatFallbackReturnsNonExecutable, same file at 0644: 'LookPath rejected it: exec: "/tmp/.../chrome-no-x": permission denied', findChrome still returns that path, and 'every chromeShot on this bin fails: fork/exec /tmp/.../chrome-no-x: permission denied'. TestJudge75_StatFallbackReturnsBareNameUnrunnable, PATH set to an empty dir, CHROME empty, cwd holding an executable ./chromium: findChrome returns "chromium" and 'exec.Command re-resolves it on PATH and fails: exec: "chromium": executable file not found in $PATH'.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-75-findchrome-stat-fallback_test.go

## .github/workflows/pr-preview.yml:64 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/.github/workflows/pr-preview.yml#L64) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: reach
TL;DR: a changed path git has to quote is dropped from the plan, so its realm gets no preview page and nothing says so
Check: run: in a scratch repo, commit a file whose name carries a non-ASCII byte and run `git diff --name-only HEAD~1 HEAD` -- observe the quoted form; then feed that exact line to `gnopreview plan -changed -` against a tree holding that realm and observe an empty realms list
Details: `git diff --name-only` C-quotes any path outside printable ASCII (core.quotePath defaults true) and always quotes one holding a quote, backslash or newline, writing `"examples/gno.land/r/x/caf/caf\303\251.gno"` into changed.txt. readLines (main.go:306-324) only does `strings.TrimSpace`, so the quotes survive, and both prefix tests in BuildPlan — the gnowebPaths loop at plan.go:212-216 and `strings.HasPrefix(f, pkgRoot+"/")` at plan.go:217 — fail against a string starting with `"`, dropping the file before byDir is consulted. Nothing reaches it today: `git ls-files examples/` is 1915 paths and none carries a byte outside 0x20-0x7e, which is why this is a latent risk rather than a live defect. Fix: `git -c core.quotePath=false diff --name-only`, or `-z` with a NUL split in readLines.
Evidence: Read plus the finder's git measurement, rechecked. plan.go:22 `pkgRoot = examplesRel + "/gno.land"`; plan.go:206 `f = filepath.ToSlash(strings.TrimSpace(f))`; plan.go:217 `if !strings.HasPrefix(f, pkgRoot+"/") || !renderRelevant(f) { continue }`. Latency measured here: `git ls-files examples/ | LC_ALL=C grep -P '[^\x20-\x7e]'` returns nothing over 1915 tracked files.

Details: comment.go:56-59 allocates and fills direct in the default branch, and it is read at one site, comment.go:69 !direct[r]. contains(p.ChangedRealms, r), already in the package at plan.go:335 and already used by isSeed, says the same thing in the line that asks, and the map with its loop goes: 196 -> 192. Two of the candidate's qualifications are loose and neither changes the call: the map sits inside the default case, so the gnoweb branch never builds it, and the single read site executes once per p.Realms entry rather than once. ## misc/gnopreview/comment.go:117 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/comment.go#L117) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: the reviewer cannot open the before page the README promises, and pays for it in the 40 MiB preview budget
Check: grep -rn '_before' misc/gnopreview/*.go returns three hits, all declarations (main.go:224 doc, crawl.go:54 doc, shots.go:27 const); grep -n 'Before' misc/gnopreview/comment.go returns 106 and 119, both the PNG path, never a page link; ShotPair (shots.go:34-44) has no field for the before page, while ScreenshotPairs computes beforeFile and discards it after the screenshot
Details: comment.go:117-119 renders the before cell as a bare `<img src="%s/%s" ...>` while comment.go:120-121 wraps only the after image in `<a href>`, and the href it uses is p.URL, which shots.go:77 fills with `path.Dir(afterFile) + "/"` - the after page. Index() at comment.go:166-177 iterates p.Realms against the head crawler's c.pages, so no _before entry appears there either, and ShotPair (shots.go:34-44) carries no field for the before page at all. README.md:73-74 says _before/ exists "so a reviewer can click through to the before page and not just its screenshot", which nothing in the generated output makes possible. Fix: add a BeforeURL field to ShotPair, fill it from path.Dir(beforeFile), and wrap the before cell's img in that href.

## misc/gnopreview/comment.go:121 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/comment.go#L121) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: every closed PR keeps a permanent comment of broken image boxes, and no job can repair it
Check: read .github/workflows/pr-preview-cleanup.yml: `permissions: {}` on both the workflow and the job, `rm -rf "pr-$PR"` as its only action, and no gh api call against issues/comments anywhere in it
Details: comment.go:90 writes the footer "Rebuilt on every push to this PR; removed when PR #%s closes", and pr-preview-cleanup.yml does exactly that with `rm -rf "pr-$PR"` as its only mutation. Every <img src="<base>/..."> that pairGrid (comment.go:118, 121) and shotGrid (comment.go:141) wrote then 404s in the PR's permanent history. The cleanup job carries `permissions: {}` at both the workflow and the job level and holds only the previews deploy key; grep -c 'gh api' over that file returns 0, so it cannot rewrite the comment it invalidates. Fix: have the cleanup job PATCH the sticky comment down to a one-line 'preview removed' note, which needs `issues: write` and the marker lookup the publish job already implements.
Evidence: read of .github/workflows/pr-preview-cleanup.yml whole: `permissions: {}` twice (workflow and job), the Remove pr-<N> step's only mutation is `rm -rf "pr-$PR"`, and grep -c 'gh api' -> 0. Footer at comment.go:90.

State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: the snapshot's tab layout is spelled four times in comment.go and once in urlToFile, and no test ties them together
Check: grep -n '_t/' misc/gnopreview/comment.go (two lines, four occurrences) against grep -n '"_t"' misc/gnopreview/crawl.go (one line, 503, inside urlToFile); then confirm no test asserts the two agree: grep -n '_t/' misc/gnopreview/*_test.go
Details: urlToFile owns the mapping from a gnoweb path to a static file and is the only place that writes the '_t' segment (crawl.go:503). comment.go never asks it for the tab pages: tabs at line 154 concatenates '/_t/source/' and '/_t/help/' onto the base URL, and Index at line 174 concatenates the same two suffixes onto a file path, four hand-spelled copies of a layout one function decides. The coupling test I added fails under that mutation ('tabs() has no link to "https://e.test/pr-1/r/gnoland/home/_tab/source/"') and passes at head. Rewrite measured: comment.go 196 -> 202 lines, go vet clean, TestCommentRealms — which pins https://example.test/pr-7/r/x/leaf/_t/source/ — still PASS, so the output is unchanged.

State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: every over-budget source page is still rendered in full by gnodev and then thrown away, so the file budget costs render time it does not save
Check: read Run lines 129-141 (getRetry, then the 200 check, then chargeFile) against wantFile line 231; then crawl_test.go:309-313, which loops inScope five times with FileBudget 1 and requires every call to return true. A counting check: point a Crawler at an httptest server whose $source page links 12 file= URLs, FileBudget 2, and count the server's request log — 12 requests, 2 pages in c.pages.
Details: Run fetches before it charges: crawl.go:129 `body, code, err := c.getRetry(u)`, then the 200 check, then crawl.go:138 `if !c.chargeFile(u) { ... continue }`. wantFile at crawl.go:231 reads `c.fileBudget[realm] < c.FileBudget`, a counter only chargeFile advances, and it is consulted at link-enqueue time (crawl.go:148-150), so a $source overview that links N files queues all N in one burst before any capture has charged a slot. Each is then fetched whole from gnodev, chroma highlighting included, and dropped. Fix: re-test the budget at dequeue time, before getRetry, and keep chargeFile for the capture that survives.
Evidence: read at head: crawl.go:129-141 orders getRetry, the status check, chargeFile in that sequence; crawl.go:231 `return c.fileBudget[realm] < c.FileBudget`; crawl_test.go:304-315 TestFileBudgetChargedOnCaptureNotDiscovery asserts inScope returns true five times under FileBudget 1, pinning the unbounded-discovery behaviour rather than bounding it. The 12-request count was not run here.

State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + claims + refactor
TL;DR: the sentinel that promises to catch js/index.js losing its /public/js/controller- prefix counts /public/ across the whole asset tree, so any other asset URL keeps it silent
Check: Count the literals in the shipped tree (grep -rc '/public/' gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/public), then read crawl.go:314-316: the n==0 branch has no return and no error. | grep -rc '/public/' gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/public --include='*.js' --include='*.css' (one file, count 1, js/index.js); then read copyTree line 573: `if n := strings.Count(string(b), "/public/"); n > 0 { rewrites += n }` — the counter is not scoped to the controller prefix the comment names. | grep -rc '/public/' gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/public/js/index.js gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/public/main.css (1 and 0, so the whole-tree count is index.js's count); then make the guard count '/public/js/controller-' in js/index.js and re-run cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./...
Details: copyTree (crawl.go:565-575) returns strings.Count(b, "/public/") summed over every .js and .css it copies; Write warns only on that total being zero (crawl.go:314-316), with a message naming js/index.js and the controller prefix. Measured on the shipped tree: total=1, perFile=map[js/index.js:1], so the sentinel equals the invariant by coincidence today. A fixture where index.js's specifier is rewritten to a bare "./js/controller-" and main.css gains url(/public/imgs/gnoland.svg) gives rewrites=1 and no warning. The n==0 branch also has no return: Write reports success either way, so even the coincidental warning cannot redden the job. Fix: count the string the comment names in the file it names, and return an error rather than a stderr line.
Evidence: go test -run TestJudge180 -v . in misc/gnopreview at ecf7af0: TestJudge180ShippedTreeHasOneOccurrence logs 'total=1 perFile=map[js/index.js:1]' PASS; TestJudge180SentinelMissesControllerPrefixLoss logs 'copyTree rewrites=1' with the controller prefix removed, FAIL. Read: crawl.go:314 'if n == 0 { fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, ...) }' has no return and Write ends on 'return c.writePages(dir)'. Merge base has no misc/gnopreview at all (ls: No such file or directory), so the diff introduces it.

## misc/gnopreview/crawl.go:444 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/crawl.go#L444) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + lines + reach
TL;DR: one oversized realm render can take the whole preview job down, since nothing bounds bytes and every captured body stays in memory to the end
Check: Run tests/b2-catalog-r2-crawl-body-size-cap.go as misc/gnopreview/zz_bodycap_test.go: serve 8 MiB per realm to a 4-realm crawl under MaxPages 400 and compare retained bytes and heap delta against realms*pageBytes. | grep -rnE 'LimitReader|MaxBytes|ContentLength|maxSize|max-size' misc/gnopreview — expect a byte cap, observe none; then confirm the only cap flag is -max-pages at main.go:74 | grep -n 'LimitReader\|MaxBytes\|maxBody\|ContentLength' misc/gnopreview/crawl.go misc/gnopreview/main.go (empty), and grep -n -i 'cap\|size\|limit' misc/gnopreview/README.md to read what Bounds claims
Details: crawl.go:444 is `b, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)` with no wrapper, and a grep for LimitReader, MaxBytes, ContentLength, maxBody, maxSize and max-size across every .go file in misc/gnopreview returns nothing. Each body is stored whole at crawl.go:143 `p := &page{... Body: body}` into c.pages and freed by nothing before Write, so peak memory is page count times page size with only the count capped. README.md's Bounds section (line 106 onward) offers -max-pages as its one quantitative lever and otherwise excludes shapes by weight. Fix: wrap the body in an io.LimitReader at a documented cap, count bytes against a total budget alongside MaxPages, and say in Bounds what the byte cap is.
Evidence: grep -rnE 'LimitReader|MaxBytes|ContentLength|maxBody|max-size|maxSize' misc/gnopreview/*.go -> NONE. crawl.go:443-445 reads the body whole with only the 30s client Timeout above it. Heap was not measured here.

## misc/gnopreview/crawl_test.go:276 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/crawl_test.go#L276) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor + tests
TL;DR: the one assertion on the slug length cap is 7 chars looser than anything slug() can emit
Check: in misc/gnopreview replace both `hex.EncodeToString(sum[:4])` in crawl.go with `sum[:6]` and run `go test -run TestURLToFileIsSafe ./...`: expect red, observe ok; then set the bound to maxSlugLen+9 and repeat | `sed -i 's/^const maxSlugLen = 64/const maxSlugLen = 400/' misc/gnopreview/crawl.go && cd misc/gnopreview && go test -run TestURLToFileIsSafe ./...` — expect a failure, observe `ok`; then `mkdir <400 z's>` for the filesystem half
Details: slug() truncates to maxSlugLen and appends "-" plus 8 hex chars, so its longest segment is 73 = maxSlugLen+9, while crawl_test.go:276 allows maxSlugLen+16 = 80. Widening the digest to sha256[:6] puts the segment at 77: only TestURLToFile goes red, on its hardcoded digests, and TestURLToFileIsSafe — the test whose whole subject is that a long argument produces no unbounded segment — reports ok. The bound is also written against maxSlugLen itself, so raising maxSlugLen to 400 raises the bound with it and the assertion still passes. One line for one line. Fix: bound each segment by maxSlugLen+9 at crawl_test.go:276.
Evidence: Head worktree at ecf7af0f, misc/gnopreview. Probe: `maxSlugLen=64 longest segment=73 test bound=maxSlugLen+16=80`. With both `hex.EncodeToString(sum[:4])` widened to `sum[:6]` the segment is 77 and `go test ./...` reports `--- FAIL: TestURLToFile` alone, while `go test -run TestURLToFileIsSafe .` reports `ok ... 0.002s` — the finder's evidence also names TestMapURL, which cannot fail here: mapURL never calls urlToFile, it reads the literal File values of its own pages map. With the bound at maxSlugLen+9 the same mutation fails on `crawl_test.go:277: segment "zzzz…-6768a45ee86c" is 77 chars`, and with the digest restored the package is `ok 0.004s`. With `const maxSlugLen = 400` and the +16 bound, `go test -run TestURLToFileIsSafe .` is `ok`. No base run: misc/gnopreview does not exist at bbd9b2ff.

## misc/gnopreview/crawl_test.go:293 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/crawl_test.go#L293) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: TestSeedsSkipSingleSegmentDirs stays green on a Seeds() that emits no directory page at all
Check: in misc/gnopreview change crawl.go's `strings.Count(d, "/") >= 2` to `>= 3` and run `go test ./...`: expect red, observe ok; then replace the loop with `reflect.DeepEqual(c.Seeds(), []string{"/r/gnoland/home", "/r/gnoland/home$source", "/r/gnoland/home$help", "/r/gnoland"})` and repeat
Details: Seeds() yields exactly one directory page per realm through `for d := path.Dir(u); strings.Count(d, "/") >= 2; d = path.Dir(d)`. Moving that bound to `>= 3` drops it: Seeds() returns three entries instead of four and the whole package still reports ok, because the test only looks for "/r" and "/p", both absent whether the directory page was seeded or silently dropped. Under that mutation every /r/<namespace> listing falls through to the live site instead of the snapshot and no test says so. Both line counts: the assertion is 5 lines and the one-equality form is 4, the function 10 lines to 9. Fix: replace the loop with one reflect.DeepEqual against the four seeds Seeds() returns, which rejects the over-seed the test names and the under-seed it cannot see.
Evidence: Head worktree at ecf7af0f, misc/gnopreview: probe prints Seeds()=["/r/gnoland/home" "/r/gnoland/home$source" "/r/gnoland/home$help" "/r/gnoland"]. With `>= 3` the probe prints the first three only and `go test ./...` prints `ok github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview 0.004s`. With the DeepEqual form in place the same mutation fails: `crawl_test.go:299: Seeds() = ["/r/gnoland/home" "/r/gnoland/home$source" "/r/gnoland/home$help"]; want [... "/r/gnoland"]`, and unmutated it is ok 0.004s. The finder's artifact reruns green: `--- PASS: TestB9SeedsAssertionIsOneSided`. No base run: misc/gnopreview does not exist at bbd9b2ff.

## misc/gnopreview/main.go:63 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/main.go#L63) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: plan accepts and advertises ten flags it never reads, so a misplaced render flag is silently ignored
Check: go build -o /tmp/gnopreview . && /tmp/gnopreview plan -h  (expect only -root/-changed/-max-realms/-live; observe all thirteen) && /tmp/gnopreview plan -chrome /nonexistent -port 1 -out /nope -root <tree> -changed /dev/null
Details: run() builds one FlagSet before it switches on the subcommand (main.go:63-79), so plan registers all thirteen flags. plan reads exactly three of them: -root and -changed feed findRoot/readLines, -max-realms feeds BuildPlan(cfg.root, changed, cfg.maxRealms) at main.go:91. The other ten (-out, -gnodev, -port, -live, -base-root, -base-url, -pr, -max-pages, -chrome, -timeout) are read only inside render(). The finder named eight; -live and -max-pages are unread by plan too. A CI author who puts a render flag on the plan step, or misspells its value, gets exit 0 and no warning, and which flags a subcommand honours is discoverable only by reading render(). Fix: register the shared flags (-root, -changed, -max-realms) on both, and the render-only ten on the render FlagSet alone, after the switch on cmd.
Evidence: Built from the branch source: cd misc/gnopreview && go build -o /tmp/gnopreview . Then `gnopreview plan -h` prints -base-root, -base-url, -chrome, -gnodev, -live, -max-pages, -out, -port, -pr, -timeout beside -root/-changed/-max-realms; and `gnopreview plan -chrome /nonexistent -port 1 -timeout 1s -out /nope -base-url http://x -pr 9 -gnodev /nonexistent -base-root /nope -root <tree> -changed /dev/null` answers {"gnoweb":false,...,"realms":[],"dropped":0,"dirs":null} with exit=0.

## misc/gnopreview/main.go:177 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/main.go#L177) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + claims + reach + refactor
TL;DR: the gnodev logs are written into -out, the directory that gets published, and only a hardcoded rm in another repository file keeps them off the site
Check: grep -n 'rm -rf _preview' .github/workflows/pr-preview.yml — the workflow carries the cleanup the tool should not need | grep -n 'os.Chdir' contribs/gnodev/command_local.go and the -home registration in contribs/gnodev/app_config.go:63; then run `gnopreview render -out _preview` from the repo root and `ls examples/_preview/` | read contribs/gnodev/command_local.go:87-89, os.Chdir(cfg.chdir) before the config is consumed; then run gnopreview render -out _preview from a gno root and ls <root>/examples/_preview | grep -n 'rm -rf _preview' .github/workflows/pr-preview.yml, then grep -n 'cfg.out' misc/gnopreview/main.go to list what render writes there
Details: startGnodev creates its log at filepath.Join(cfg.out, logName) (main.go:183), so a render leaves gnodev.log — and gnodev-base.log when a base pass runs — inside the tree pr-preview.yml uploads as the artifact. The only thing removing them is `rm -rf _preview/gnodev.log _preview/gnodev-base.log _preview/.gnodev-*` at pr-preview.yml:103, three hardcoded names in a different file: a renamed log, a fourth scratch file, or any second caller publishes them. The keybase half of the claim is refuted: gnodev never creates the -home directory. setup_address_book.go:18 reads `} else if !osm.DirExists(cfg.home) {` and only warns, so -home is import-only and nothing named .gnodev-<port> appears in -out or anywhere else. The chdir mechanism is real — gnopreview passes -home as the relative `_preview/.gnodev-8899` beside `-C <root>/examples`, and execLocalApp does os.Chdir(cfg.chdir) at command_local.go:87-89 before the config is consumed — but it makes the flag inert rather than misplacing a directory, which also means the collision main.go:173-176 says it is avoiding ($GNOHOME shared by the two passes) cannot happen. Fix: give render a work dir of its own (os.MkdirTemp or a -work flag) for both logs, so -out holds only what is meant to be served, and drop -home with the paragraph justifying it.
Evidence: tests/judge-173-out-scratch.sh, run at head with a stub gnodev that records its argv: '--- what landed in the published directory ---' lists gnodev.log; the argv shows 'ARG=-home' / 'ARG=_preview/.gnodev-8899' then 'ARG=-C' / '<root>/examples'; and the find over _preview and examples for '.gnodev-*' prints no line, at head or after the run.

State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: a pull request that only changes the preview tool renders no preview, so the crawler's own change is never exercised
Check: read .github/workflows/pr-preview.yml paths against gnowebPaths, then run gnopreview plan with changed=misc/gnopreview/crawl.go and read the emitted JSON: gnoweb false, realms empty
Details: pr-preview.yml triggers on misc/gnopreview/** and on the workflow file itself (lines 24-26), while gnowebPaths carries only gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/, gno.land/cmd/gnoweb/ and contribs/gnodev/ (plan.go:28-32). A change to the crawler, the slugger or the comment writer therefore sets Gnoweb false with no changed realm, and the job stops at the skip gate (pr-preview.yml:79-82). It is not a wasted run — the step still builds the binary and runs plan (line 77), so the tool is compile-checked — but no crawl, no snapshot and no comment ever runs against the changed tool. Fix: add "misc/gnopreview/" to gnowebPaths so a change to the tool renders the seed sample.
Evidence: pr-preview.yml:24-26 `- misc/gnopreview/**`, `- .github/workflows/pr-preview.yml`; plan.go:28-32 gnowebPaths lists three trees, neither of those among them; plan.go:212-215 sets Gnoweb only on a gnowebPaths prefix; pr-preview.yml:79-82 sets skip=true when gnoweb is false and realms is empty.

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:58 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L58) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + lines + refactor + tests
TL;DR: Pkg.Draft is written for every package and read by nothing, so the field reads as a filter the tool does not have
Check: grep -rn '\.Draft' misc/gnopreview | grep -v 'plan.go:58\|plan.go:142' | grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview/*.go  (one declaration, one assignment, no read) | grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview (assignment only, no read) and grep -rl '^draft = true' examples --include=gnomod.toml | grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview/*.go (three sites, no reader); grep -rl 'draft *= *true' examples/gno.land/ | wc -l vs grep -rl 'ignore *= *true' examples/gno.land/ | wc -l | `grep -n '\.Draft' misc/gnopreview/*.go` (only the declaration and the assignment), then delete the draft arm of modFlags and run `cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./...` | grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview -- only plan.go:58 (the declaration) and plan.go:142 (the write) match, no read | git apply reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-draft-field.patch at ecf7af0, then cd misc/gnopreview && gofmt -l . && go vet ./... && go test ./...; and grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview for the read that does not exist
Details: Draft appears three times in the module and never as a read: the declaration at plan.go:58, the write at plan.go:142 (pkg.Draft, pkg.Ignore = modFlags(...)), and the `case "draft=true"` arm plus the first return value of modFlags at plan.go:155-168 that feed it. Its sibling Ignore is read twice in the same file, plan.go:218 (`p != nil && !p.Ignore`) and plan.go:242 (`pkgs[p].Ignore`), and plan_test.go covers `ignore = true` at lines 45-47 with no draft case, so nothing notices. The reader's cost is the wrong half: every gnomod.toml under examples/ is scanned for `draft = true` and the answer discarded, while the field's presence makes the next reader prove draft realms are not filtered like ignored ones. The finder's second framing, that the draft filter is the missing one, is not settled here: the tree holds one draft realm, examples/gno.land/r/demo/draftrealm, and the only draft rejection in the chain is gno.land/pkg/sdk/vm/keeper.go:668 `if gm.Draft && height > 0`, which leaves genesis loading alone, so whether a gnodev preview would refuse it needs a boot.
Evidence: grep -rn 'Draft' misc/gnopreview --include='*.go' at ecf7af0 returns exactly plan.go:58 (`Draft   bool     // gnomod.toml: draft = true`) and plan.go:142, no read. Finder's patch applied in my own scratch worktree: git diff --numstat = `8	12	misc/gnopreview/plan.go` (12 lines -> 8), gofmt -l . silent, go vet ./... silent, `ok  github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview	0.004s`; unpatched baseline at the same head the same ok line. Merge base bbd9b2f has no misc/gnopreview directory at all, so this is new code, not a regression.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-draft-field.patch

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:66 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L66) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: Pkg.Dir is always "examples/"+Pkg.Path and byDir re-indexes a map that already answers; both go, 345 -> 337 lines
Check: in a clone at ecf7af0f2, apply edit list A from the header of the artifact (drop the Dir field, drop Dir: rel, drop the byDir block, rewrite the two lookups), then cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./...
Details: LoadPkgs sets Path and Dir from one string at plan.go:138-139, `Path: strings.TrimPrefix(rel, examplesRel+"/")` and `Dir: rel`, and the walk is rooted at pkgRoot = "examples/gno.land", so Dir == "examples/"+Path for every package with no exception. BuildPlan then spends five lines at 199-201 building byDir to invert a map it already holds, and its two consumers reduce to direct lookups: byDir[path.Dir(f)] is pkgs[strings.TrimPrefix(path.Dir(f), examplesRel+"/")], and pkgs[r].Dir is examplesRel+"/"+r. The cost of keeping both is two fields that can disagree and a second index every later reader has to prove is in sync. Fix: apply edit list A — dropping the field also proves no caller outside plan.go reads it, since the package and its tests still compile.
Evidence: Applied edit list A in a detached worktree at ecf7af0f2 (5 replacements, each asserted unique): wc -l plan.go 345 -> 337; gofmt -l . silent; go vet ./... silent; 'ok  github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview  0.004s'. The compile itself is the reach check: removing `Dir` from the Pkg struct breaks nothing in the package or its tests.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-r2-plan-shape_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:75 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L75) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: the Realms/Dirs pairing the before/after pass depends on is neither documented nor tested
Check: grep -n 'plan.Dirs' misc/gnopreview/*.go and read main.go:237-247; confirm no test asserts len(Dirs) == len(Realms) or their pairing
Details: main.go:241 and main.go:246 index plan.Dirs by the loop variable of `for i, r := range plan.Realms`, so renderBase stats and loads plan.Dirs[i] as realm r. BuildPlan appends both in one loop (plan.go:271-273) and the invariant holds at this head, measured len(Realms)=26 and len(Dirs)=26 on a gnoweb-plus-ufmt plan. Neither field's doc comment states the pairing and nothing asserts it: grep for Dirs across plan_test.go and crawl_test.go returns nothing. A later filter applied to Realms or Dirs alone would pair a realm with another realm's directory and render the wrong package from the merge base with no error anywhere. ## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:91 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L91) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: reach + catalog
TL;DR: a seed realm that gets renamed leaves the gnoweb preview with no error and no note, and losing all four would crash the job instead of skipping it
Check: fixture repo with no seed realm, BuildPlan(root, ["gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/app.go"], 25): expect Empty() true or a realm, observe Empty() false with zero realms and urlOf(Realms[0]) panicking | BuildPlan on a root whose examples tree carries none of the four seed realms with changed=[gno.land/pkg/gnoweb/public/main.css]: expect Empty() true or a guarded render, observe Empty() false and plan.Realms empty
Details: Empty() at plan.go:91 tests `!p.Gnoweb && len(p.Realms) == 0`, so a gnoweb plan is non-empty whatever Realms holds, and render() runs `urlOf(plan.Realms[0])` at main.go:112 right after that guard. The seeds are four hardcoded paths (plan.go:37-42) added only when `pkgs[r] != nil` (plan.go:265), with no error, no log and no Dropped increment when one does not resolve. All four resolve at this head, so the index is unreachable in CI today and the live half is the silent drop: one rename costs a sample page from every gnoweb preview, and nothing in the run or the comment says so. Fix: report an unresolvable seed, and make Empty() also cover len(Realms)==0 so the job prints "nothing to preview" rather than panicking.
Evidence: My own tests/judge-115-gnoweb-seed-drift_test.go, run at ecf7af0f2 in a detached scratch worktree, `go test -run TestJudge115 -v .`, all three PASS: TestJudge115SeedsStillResolve passes against the real ../../examples, so no current input reaches the index; TestJudge115RenamedSeedIsDroppedSilently logs `Realms=[gno.land/r/docs/security_patterns gno.land/r/gnoland/blog gno.land/r/gnoland/boards2/v0] Dropped=0`, the fourth seed gone with no error; TestJudge115AllSeedsGonePanics logs `Gnoweb=true Realms=[] Empty()=false` then `render() panics past Empty(): runtime error: index out of range [0] with length 0`. The finder's tests/b7-reach-plan-caps.go reproduces the same panic (TestB7NonEmptyPlanWithNoRealms FAIL). plan_test.go:108-113 pins that state as expected (`wantRealms: []string{}`, `wantMode: "gnoweb"`). Merge base carries no misc/gnopreview.
Artifact: tests/judge-115-gnoweb-seed-drift_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:114 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L114) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + lines + reach
TL;DR: the dotted-directory guard drops that directory's own package and still walks into every child
Check: read filepath.WalkDir's contract: only fs.SkipDir prunes a directory; confirm no caller of LoadPkgs relies on descending into dot directories | read filepath.WalkDir's contract for a nil return from a directory entry, then find examples/gno.land -type d -name '.*' | find examples/gno.land -type d -name '.*' | wc -l -> 0; mkdir examples/gno.land/.hidden/r/x/z with a gnomod.toml and a lib.gno and LoadPkgs returns it.
Details: plan.go:114 is `if !d.IsDir() || strings.HasPrefix(d.Name(), ".") { return nil }`. Returning nil from a filepath.WalkDir callback on a directory means continue; only fs.SkipDir prunes the subtree, so the guard skips the dotted directory as a package and descends into it anyway. I reran the finder's artifact in a scratch worktree at ecf7af0: a package at examples/gno.land/r/.hidden/deep is loaded while examples/gno.land/r/.self is dropped, which is the inconsistency in one run. Nothing renders wrong today, since `find examples/gno.land -type d -name '.*'` is empty at this head, so what it costs is a package under a future dotted directory being previewed and booted in gnodev against the guard's stated intent. Fix: return fs.SkipDir instead of nil on the dotted-directory branch, split from the `!d.IsDir()` branch, which still returns nil.
Evidence: head, scratch worktree at ecf7af0, `go test -run TestDotDirGuardStillDescends -v .` in misc/gnopreview: `--- PASS: TestDotDirGuardStillDescends`, `zz_b7_test.go:83: LOADED gno.land/r/.hidden/deep: the walk descended past the dotted directory`, `zz_b7_test.go:88: SKIPPED gno.land/r/.self: the dotted directory's own package is dropped`. Merge base: `ls .../gno-review-6194-base/misc/gnopreview` -> No such file or directory, so the whole behaviour arrives with this diff.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-r2-plan-shape_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:114 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L114) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: lines + catalog + reach
TL;DR: a package under a dot-directory would be loaded and previewed, despite the guard that reads as excluding one
Check: read filepath.WalkDir's contract for a nil return on a directory entry, then `find examples/gno.land -type d -name '.*'`
Details: filepath.WalkDir prunes a directory only on an fs.SkipDir return; the guard at plan.go:114 returns nil, which drops the dotted directory entry itself and still descends into everything under it. A package at examples/gno.land/r/.staging/foo, a gnomod.toml plus one .gno file, would therefore be registered in pkgs, enter the dependents graph and be rendered. Nothing under examples/gno.land is dot-prefixed today, so the guard protects nothing and misstates what it does; it is a latent risk, not a live defect. Fix: return fs.SkipDir for a dot-prefixed directory, keeping return nil for the non-directory case.
Evidence: plan.go:114-116 `if !d.IsDir() || strings.HasPrefix(d.Name(), ".") { return nil }`; find <head>/examples/gno.land -type d -name '.*' | wc -l -> 0.

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:138 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L138) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: reach
TL;DR: a package whose module line differs from its directory previews zero realms
Check: for each examples/gno.land/**/gnomod.toml compare the module line against the directory: 145 files, 0 mismatches today. Make one mismatch in fakeRepo and BuildPlan drops the dependent.
Details: LoadPkgs derives Pkg.Path from the directory (plan.go:138) while dependents() keys the reverse graph by the import strings found in source (plan.go:299-300), so the two only meet as long as module equals directory. Built both shapes in a temp tree: with module = gno.land/p/x/base declared under .../base/v2, a realm importing gno.land/p/x/base gives Realms=[]; with the module line matching the directory the same realm gives Realms=[gno.land/r/x/leaf]. Latent at this head, confirmed independently at 145 gnomod.toml files under examples/gno.land and 0 whose module differs from its directory, and plan_test.go:29 writes the two equal in every fixture, so no test would catch the day it stops holding. Fix: read the module path out of gnomod.toml and key Pkg.Path on it, or assert module == directory once in LoadPkgs.
Evidence: judge harness: C252 mismatch: ChangedPkgs=[gno.land/p/x/base/v2] Realms=[]   <- leaf imports gno.land/p/x/base / C252 aligned:  ChangedPkgs=[gno.land/p/x/base/v2] Realms=[gno.land/r/x/leaf]. Tree scan: 145 gnomod.toml under examples/gno.land, 0 mismatches.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-2-plan-reads_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:155 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L155) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog
TL;DR: an ignored realm whose ignore line carries a comment or a tab is loaded and previewed anyway
Check: cd misc/gnopreview && go test -count=1 -v -run TestModFlagsInlineComment
Details: modFlags strips every space and compares the whole line against `ignore=true` (plan.go:161-166), so anything after the value defeats it. Re-ran the finder's case and widened it: `ignore = true` reads ignore=true, `ignore = true # quarantined until X` reads ignore=false, `draft = true # not ready` reads draft=false, and a tab-separated `ignore\t=\ttrue` reads ignore=false. Such a realm then passes the !p.Ignore guards at plan.go:220 and plan.go:241 and is handed to gnodev as a dir to load, which is the outcome the flag exists to prevent. Latent: no gnomod.toml under examples/ carries a comment on either line today. Fix: parse with gnovm/pkg/gnomod, whose File already declares Ignore with a toml:"ignore,omitempty" tag at file.go:21.
Evidence: judge harness: C254 "ignore = true" -> ignore=true / C254 "ignore = true # quarantined until X" -> ignore=false / C254 "draft = true # not ready" -> draft=false / C254 "ignore\t=\ttrue" -> ignore=false. grep for a commented draft/ignore line across examples/: none.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-2-plan-reads_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:206 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L206) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: changedFiles is a map of maps deduping base names that cannot collide
Check: apply edit list B from the artifact header, then cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./...
Details: changedFiles at plan.go:206 is map[string]map[string]bool, costing a nil check, an inner map allocation per changed package and a sortedKeys call to get back to the []string the Plan field holds. Its keys are path.Base(f) for f in one package directory, which collide only when the same path appears twice in the changed-file list. I applied edit list B in a scratch worktree and pinned the output for a list carrying a duplicate entry, two .gno files, a gnomod.toml and a _test.gno: byte-identical both ways, and the package suite stays green. plan.go goes 345 -> 343 lines and loses one allocation per changed package. Fix: changedFiles := map[string][]string{}, append path.Base(f), and sort.Strings(f) then slices.Compact(f) where sortedKeys(f) is called at plan.go:274.
Evidence: head, scratch worktree at ecf7af0, misc/gnopreview. Before (map of maps): `ChangedFiles={"gno.land/r/demo/a":["b.gno","gnomod.toml","z.gno"]}` PASS. After edit list B: `wc -l plan.go` -> `343 plan.go`, same line `ChangedFiles={"gno.land/r/demo/a":["b.gno","gnomod.toml","z.gno"]}` PASS, and `go test ./...` -> `ok  github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview  0.004s`. plan.go restored to 345 lines afterwards. Merge base has no misc/gnopreview.

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:225 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L225) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: claims
TL;DR: a delete-only PR gets no per-file source page, only the name of a file gnoweb no longer serves
Check: read crawl.go:227-232 against BuildPlan's changedFiles population, then BuildPlan(root, ["examples/gno.land/r/gnoland/home/deleted.gno"], 0) and check ChangedFiles for a name no file carries
Details: BuildPlan records path.Base(f) for every changed path, deletions included, because byDir still resolves the surviving directory (plan.go:220-225). Crawler.wantFile then returns true only for names in that set (crawl.go:227-230), so the set of wanted files and the set of files on disk are disjoint when the PR only removed one. A reviewer opening the preview of such a PR gets the realm render and the source index and not one per-file source page, exactly where they came to see what the deletion left behind. Fix: skip a changed path whose file is absent from the head tree before recording it in changedFiles, and fall back to the gnoweb file budget when a realm's set comes out empty.
Evidence: judge harness at the head tree: C224 ChangedFiles=map[gno.land/r/gnoland/home:[deleted.gno]] / C224 wantFile(home, home.gno) = false   <- on disk / C224 wantFile(home, deleted.gno) = true   <- gnoweb serves no such page
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-2-plan-reads_test.go

TL;DR: the cap keeps an archived realm and cuts every r/sys realm, decided by name
Check: diff the realm sets of the capped and uncapped plans for one changed package: gnopreview plan -root <head> -changed - (default cap 25) against -max-realms 0
Details: sort.SliceStable's comparator carries one key, changedPkgs[realm] (plan.go:253-255), so among the indirectly affected realms the order stays the alphabetical one sortedKeys produced and realms[:maxRealms] keeps the alphabetic head. Reproduced against the head tree rather than through the prebuilt binary, which is not on this PATH: a change to gno.land/p/nt/ufmt/v0 affects 31 realms, the 25 kept start at gno.land/r/archive/boards, and the 6 cut are exactly the system realms. The reviewer of a widely imported package gets archived realms previewed while the system realms are dropped, and the comment tells them how many went and never which. ## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:284 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L284) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: the _test.gno / _filetest.gno predicate is spelled twice and only one of the two sites has a test
Check: extract isTestSource and call it from both switches, then cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./... (TestRenderRelevant and TestBuildPlan/'tests and filetests do not trigger a preview' are the two that cover the pair)
Details: plan.go:128 in LoadPkgs reads `case strings.HasSuffix(e.Name(), "_test.gno"), strings.HasSuffix(e.Name(), "_filetest.gno"):` and plan.go:284 in renderRelevant reads the same pair over `base`. The two decide different things off one rule: 128 keeps a file out of a package's deployable file list (and out of gnoImports, and out of the `len(files) == 0` drop), 284 keeps a changed file from triggering a preview at plan.go:217. A third test suffix added to one site alone leaves a file that triggers a realm preview but is absent from that realm's own file list, or the reverse, and both halves still return a plausible plan so nothing fails. renderRelevant is covered by TestRenderRelevant; LoadPkgs's switch has no test of its own. Fix: one `isTestSource(name string) bool` called from both switches.
Evidence: Verified in my own worktree at ecf7af0: the two suffix pairs are byte-identical apart from the variable. I wrote the extraction (isTestSource plus its doc comment, both call sites rewritten): `gofmt -l .` silent, `go vet ./...` silent, `go test -count=1 -run 'TestRenderRelevant|TestBuildPlan' -v .` gave `--- PASS: TestRenderRelevant` and `--- PASS: TestBuildPlan` over 10 subtests including `tests_and_filetests_do_not_trigger_a_preview`, `ok github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview 0.004s`. Line counts: `git diff --numstat` = `9 2 misc/gnopreview/plan.go`, so this is not a line saving — 9 added, 2 removed, 3 of the 9 being the doc comment — the argument is the single source of truth, not size. grep confirms LoadPkgs is named only at plan.go:107 and its caller plan.go:194, never in plan_test.go.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-104-is-test-source.patch

## misc/gnopreview/plan.go:339 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan.go#L339) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: mustRel is a 7-line panicking helper with one caller and is the only reason plan.go imports fmt; inlining filepath.Rel drops 5 lines and the import
Check: git apply reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-mustrel.patch at ecf7af0, then cd misc/gnopreview && gofmt -l . && go vet ./... && go test ./...
Details: mustRel is defined at plan.go:339-345 and called once, at plan.go:136 inside LoadPkgs' WalkDir callback, which already returns error on the walk's own err (plan.go:111) and on os.ReadDir (plan.go:117). `fmt.` occurs nowhere else in plan.go: line 342, mustRel's panic message, is the file's only use, so the helper carries the import on its own. The finder's failure scenario is the part that does not hold: p is produced by filepath.WalkDir(base, ...) where base := filepath.Join(root, filepath.FromSlash(pkgRoot)) at plan.go:109-110, so every walked path is root's own path extended and cannot disagree with it on absolute versus relative, which is the only way filepath.Rel errors here. The panic is unreachable, so what the reader gains is five lines and one import fewer, not a crash removed.
Evidence: grep -rn 'mustRel\|fmt\.' misc/gnopreview/plan.go at ecf7af0: plan.go:136 (the one call), plan.go:339 (the definition), plan.go:342 (the only fmt. in the file). Finder's patch applied in my scratch worktree: git diff --numstat = `5	10	misc/gnopreview/plan.go` (10 lines -> 5) and the hunk removes `"fmt"` from the import block; gofmt -l . silent, go vet ./... silent, `ok  github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview	0.004s`. Merge base bbd9b2f has no misc/gnopreview, so the helper is new code.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b7-refactor-mustrel.patch

## misc/gnopreview/plan_test.go:23 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/plan_test.go#L23) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: the seam is mechanical and holds: planFrom lets a graph case be a Pkg literal instead of a directory tree, at +5 lines in plan.go
Check: extract the body of BuildPlan after LoadPkgs into `func planFrom(pkgs map[string]*Pkg, changed []string, maxRealms int) *Plan`, have BuildPlan call it, and run cd misc/gnopreview && go test ./...
Details: The finder ran nothing; the extraction does hold. BuildPlan splits into LoadPkgs plus planFrom with no other edit, go vet is silent and the package stays green. A rewritten case proves the payoff: the transitive-dependents case and a cap case both express as a four-entry map[string]*Pkg literal with no files on disk. The cost claim is weaker than stated -- fakeRepo is built once per TestBuildPlan, not per subtest, and all ten subtests together run in 0.005s -- so the argument is expressiveness (a draft package, a cycle, a realm count above the cap become literals), not time. Fix: extract planFrom and move the graph cases of the table onto it, keeping one fakeRepo case for LoadPkgs.
Evidence: Scratch worktree at ecf7af0: plan.go 345 -> 350 lines, `go vet ./...` silent, `ok github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview 0.004s`, and the literal-driven case `--- PASS: TestZZPlanFromLiteral (0.00s)`. plan_test.go's fakeRepo is lines 14-51 (38 lines) and TestBuildPlan holds 10 subtests, each calling BuildPlan and so LoadPkgs once.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:24 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L24) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: reach
TL;DR: every published path starts with an underscore, so the preview's images and tab pages survive only on a marker file this PR never writes
Check: grep -rn nojekyll over the PR returns only misc/gnopreview/README.md:96 (ran: it does); then curl -sI <previews site>/pr-<N>/_shots/<name>.png on a live preview, and git ls-files .nojekyll in the previews repo
Details: shotsDir is `_shots` (shots.go:24) and the tab and render-argument directories are `_t`/`_a`, so a Jekyll build on the previews site drops all of them. A grep for nojekyll across misc/gnopreview and .github/workflows returns exactly one hit, README.md:96, which states the marker is mandatory wherever the site is published - a prose dependency on an out-of-tree manual step, with no workflow step writing it and none checking it. If the previews repo is ever recreated, forked or re-pointed without it, every <img> in every sticky comment 404s while the render and publish jobs stay green. Fix: `touch .nojekyll` in the publish job before `git add -A`, which is idempotent and removes the dependency.
Evidence: grep -rni nojekyll over misc/gnopreview and .github/workflows -> misc/gnopreview/README.md:96 only. pr-preview-publish.yml:138-141 does rm -rf / mkdir -p / cp -r / git add -A and writes no dotfile. Whether gnolang/gno-previews carries the marker today was not checked - it is out of this tree.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:51 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L51) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: the two screenshot passes carry the same 15-line browser-and-server prologue, so a step added to one silently skips the other
Check: apply the rewrite's shotEnv helper and run gofmt -l . ; go vet ./... ; go test ./... in misc/gnopreview, then count non-comment lines before and after with grep -v '^[[:space:]]*//' shots.go | grep -c '[^[:space:]]'
Details: diff of shots.go:51-65 against shots.go:117-131 at head returns two hunks only: the message word ("before/after" vs "screenshots") and the serve() result name (origin vs base). Everything else — findChrome, the empty-bin bail, serve, defer Close, MkdirAll(outDir/_shots, 0o755) — is byte-identical. A Chrome flag or a temp-profile dir added for one pass therefore lands in one function and not the other, and the before/after pair and the gnoweb sample then disagree on the same runner. ## SKIP misc/gnopreview/shots.go:76 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L76) · Suggestion
State: PLAUSIBLE, band: Suggestion, angle: lines
TL;DR: the reviewer may read a before/after belonging to the previous push, with nothing in the comment saying so
Check: grep -n 'path.Dir' misc/gnopreview/shots.go misc/gnopreview/comment.go: shots.go:77 against comment.go:175-176, same mapping, two spellings
Details: The two halves the read can settle both hold: shots.go:76 `name := slug(strings.TrimPrefix(urlOf(r), "/"))` makes the shot filename a pure function of the realm path, the publish job writes it under a per-PR directory whose URL carries no sha (pr-preview-publish.yml:138-140), and the comment is edited in place at pr-preview-publish.yml:174 `gh api -X PATCH "repos/$REPO/issues/comments/$id"`. So a second push republishes new bytes at a byte-identical URL inside an unchanged comment body. Whether GitHub's image proxy then serves the previous push's PNG depends on its cache TTL, which cannot be read from this tree - that half is unverified here. Fix: put the head sha in the shot filename, or append it as a query string in the comment's img src, so each push addresses its own image.
Evidence: read at head: shots.go:76 (filename from the realm path alone), pr-preview-publish.yml:138-140 (rm -rf/mkdir/cp into pr-$PR) and :174 (PATCH of the existing comment id). No live push or cache-header check was possible from the worktree.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:77 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L77) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog
TL;DR: when the 1280x860 shot crops what changed, the reviewer has no way to read the before page's text
Check: go test -run TestChromeShotFalseSuccess/missing_page ./... in misc/gnopreview with the artifact test: expect an error from chromeShot on a 404 URL, observe nil and a written PNG
Details: ShotPair carries one page URL and shots.go:77 fills it with the after page only: `pair := ShotPair{Realm: r, URL: path.Dir(afterFile) + "/", New: newRealms[r]}`. ScreenshotPairs computes beforeFile at shots.go:89, uses it for the screenshot, and discards it; comment.go:118 emits the before cell as a bare img while 121 wraps the after one in an href, and Index() emits nothing under _before/. The published tree therefore carries a whole merge-base render that no generated link reaches, against README.md:73-74's statement that it exists for exactly that click-through. Fix: keep beforeFile on ShotPair and wrap the before cell in its href, the same shape the after cell already uses. The candidate anchors shots.go:86, which is the "New in this PR" comment; the line that sets URL is 77.
Evidence: read at head: shots.go:77 (URL from afterFile), shots.go:89 `if beforeFile, ok := base.FileOf(urlOf(r)); ok` - used only as a chromeShot argument at 90-91; comment.go:118 bare img vs 121 <a href>; comment.go:168-176 Index iterates head pages only. Same underlying fact as candidates 200 and 210.

Details: chromeShot's only verdict is shots.go:187-189, a non-empty file at the destination, so a URL the snapshot server answers 404 on returns nil: http.FileServer (shots.go:159) sends a plain-text `404 page not found` body, Chrome paints it, exits 0 and writes a real PNG. ScreenshotPairs at shots.go:78-83 then sets pair.After and the comment captions that error page as the realm. Today every afterFile comes from head.FileOf over c.pages, whose pages writePages has written, so the miss is latent rather than live - it goes live the first time a page's file name and its served path stop agreeing, through a prefix, an escape or a writeFile that failed. Fix: HEAD the URL and fail the shot on a non-200 before invoking Chrome.
Evidence: read at head: shots.go:186-190 is the whole check; shots.go:157-163 serve() hands the directory to http.FileServer, whose miss is a 200-shaped page to a browser. The finder's run recorded `chromeShot on a URL the server answers 404 on returned nil and wrote 6743 bytes`; the mechanism is settled by the read without it.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b4-catalog-r2-chromeshot-false-success_test.go

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:83 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L83) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: each screenshot's path is spelled twice, so a naming change touching one spelling publishes a comment whose image link points at a file Chrome never wrote
Check: git apply the rewrite and run, from misc/gnopreview: gofmt -l . && go vet ./... && go test ./... ; the shotFile helper returns the single relative path and hands filepath.Join(outDir, rel) to chromeShot
Details: Three sites spell one path twice: 79/83 (name+"-after.png"), 91/92 (name+"-before.png") and 138/143 (s.name+".png"), the first spelling filepath.Join(outDir, shotsDir, ...) for Chrome and the second path.Join(shotsDir, ...) for the comment. Nothing reconciles them: chromeShot's only check is os.Stat on the path it was handed (shots.go:187), so a comment path that drifted from the written path is never noticed and the PR comment renders a broken image. Fix: build the relative path once and pass filepath.Join(outDir, rel) to chromeShot, as the finder's shotFile helper does.
Evidence: Read of shots.go at head: 79 filepath.Join(outDir, shotsDir, name+"-after.png") vs 83 path.Join(shotsDir, name+"-after.png"); 91 vs 92 for -before.png; 138 vs 143 for s.name+".png". shots.go:187 'if fi, err := os.Stat(out); err != nil || fi.Size() == 0' is the only post-write check and reads chromeShot's own argument. Rewrite applied at ecf7af0: gofmt -l . no output, go vet clean, 'ok  github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview  0.005s'; non-comment lines 169 -> 170, file lines 213 -> 223. Merge base bbd9b2f has no misc/gnopreview at all.

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:167 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L167) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: refactor
TL;DR: chromeShot's filepath.Abs block is four lines Chrome and os.Stat both already resolve the same way
Check: go test -run TestChromeShotRelativeDst ./... with the block present and with it deleted, comparing the file location and size; grep -rn 'os.Chdir|cmd.Dir|\.Dir =' misc/gnopreview/*.go for a chdir anywhere in the package
Details: chromeShot passes out to two places only: --screenshot= and os.Stat. Chrome is started with exec.Command and no cmd.Dir, and nothing in the package calls os.Chdir, so the browser inherits the Go process's working directory and resolves a relative --screenshot= against exactly what os.Stat(dst) would. filepath.Abs therefore only Cleans the path, and its error return fires only when os.Getwd fails. Deleting the block takes shots.go from 213 to 209 lines with go vet clean and the package's tests green. Fix: drop the Abs block and pass dst to --screenshot= and os.Stat.
Evidence: Head, judge scratch worktree at ecf7af0f2, chrome at /usr/sbin/google-chrome-stable. grep -rn 'os.Chdir|cmd.Dir|.Dir = ' misc/gnopreview/*.go: no match. Finder's fixture rerun from the file as zz_b4_test.go: with the block, 'relative dst landed at /tmp/TestChromeShotRelativeDst473451620/001/zz-rel.png, 5654 bytes'; with the block deleted and out replaced by dst, 'relative dst landed at /tmp/TestChromeShotRelativeDst530631153/001/zz-rel.png, 5654 bytes'. Same cwd-relative landing, same byte count. 'wc -l shots.go' 213 -> 209; 'go vet .' exit 0; 'go test .' ok github.com/gnolang/gno/misc/gnopreview 0.418s.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/judge-203-chromeshot-abs.patch

## misc/gnopreview/shots.go:187 [gh](https://github.com/gnolang/gno/blob/ecf7af0f29abe4737a52803d672bc5a33c17cc60/misc/gnopreview/shots.go#L187) · Suggestion
State: CONFIRMED, band: Suggestion, angle: catalog + lines
TL;DR: a screenshot that shows an error page or a page that never painted is published as the realm's rendering
Check: go test -run TestChromeShotFalseSuccess/stale_file ./... in misc/gnopreview with the artifact test: expect an error from chromeShot, observe nil | tests/b4-lines-r2-chromeshot-silent-truncation_test.go: copy into misc/gnopreview and run go test -run TestB4ChromeShotSilentTruncation -v ./...; it fails only if the budget waits for the slow page or the 404 URL writes no image
Details: shots.go:187-189 is the whole post-run health check: `if fi, err := os.Stat(out); err != nil || fi.Size() == 0 { return fmt.Errorf("chrome wrote no image") }`. It stats the destination path, not anything this run produced, and it asks nothing about what was rendered. The snapshot is served by http.FileServer (shots.go:159), which answers a miss with a plain-text `404 page not found` body that Chrome paints and screenshots at exit 0, so chromeShot returns nil and ScreenshotPairs sets pair.After at shots.go:83; comment.go:121 then captions that image `after - this PR`. Fix: assert the run wrote the file this invocation (stat before, compare mtime or write to a fresh temp path) and check the HTTP status of the URL before shooting it.
Evidence: read at head: shots.go:184-190, the only check after cmd.CombinedOutput is os.Stat(out)+Size()==0; no status check, no content check, no per-run freshness check. shots.go:159 Handler: http.FileServer(http.Dir(dir)). The byte-identical slow/blank digests (slow=2bff94039a8c blank=2bff94039a8c) are the finder's Chrome 152 measurement, not rerun here; the missing-check mechanism is settled by the read alone.
Artifact: reviews/pr/6xxx/6194-gnoweb-pr-preview/1-ecf7af0/tests/b4-lines-r2-chromeshot-silent-truncation_test.go
